  burn: {"name":"Burn","icon":"🔥","text":"Takes 1 damage at the end of each round."}

  stagger: {"name":"Stagger","icon":"💥︁","text":"−1 Power and −1 Focus: its physical and elemental attacks each deal 1 less damage."}
